### [1] [Methodological Variation in Studying Staff and Student Perceptions of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11158)
*Juliana Gerard,Morgan Macleod,Kelly Norwood,Aisling Reid,Muskaan Singh*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 比较学生与教职员工对AI认知的不同研究方法，发现单一数据源的不同分析方法会产生不同结果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究AI认知通常使用单一量化或质化方法，或混合方法比较不同数据源，但缺乏对同一数据源不同分析方法的比较研究

Method: 收集两种质化数据：独立评论和结构化焦点小组；对每种数据源进行两种分析：情感/立场分析测量整体正负性，词云和主题分析提供详细内容信息

Result: 不同分析方法对同一数据源会产生不同结果，整体正负性测量与详细内容分析呈现不同图景

Conclusion: 研究方法的选择会影响研究结果，这对机构情境和先前研究比较具有重要启示

Abstract: In this paper, we compare methodological approaches for comparing student and staff perceptions, and ask: how much do these measures vary across different approaches? We focus on the case of AI perceptions, which are generally assessed via a single quantitative or qualitative measure, or with a mixed methods approach that compares two distinct data sources - e.g. a quantitative questionnaire with qualitative comments. To compare different approaches, we collect two forms of qualitative data: standalone comments and structured focus groups. We conduct two analyses for each data source: with a sentiment and stance analysis, we measure overall negativity/positivity of the comments and focus group conversations, respectively. Meanwhile, word clouds from the comments and a thematic analysis of the focus groups provide further detail on the content of this qualitative data - particularly the thematic analysis, which includes both similarities and differences between students and staff. We show that different analyses can produce different results - for a single data source. This variation stems from the construct being evaluated - an overall measure of positivity/negativity can produce a different picture from more detailed content-based analyses. We discuss the implications of this variation for institutional contexts, and for the comparisons from previous studies.

</details>


### [2] [BIRD: A Museum Open Dataset Combining Behavior Patterns and Identity Types to Better Model Visitors' Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11160)
*Alexanne Worm,Florian Marchal,Sylvain Castagnos*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 构建了一个结合上下文、行为和反馈数据的博物馆参观者开放数据集，用于改进个性化推荐路径


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决文化遗产领域数据缺乏的问题，特别是现有数据集多为特定模型评估而设计，无法全面建模参观者体验

Method: 2019年2-3月对51名参与者进行眼动追踪研究，让他们自由探索博物馆3层楼400多件艺术品，收集上下文数据（人口统计、偏好、参观习惯等）、行为数据（时空轨迹、注视数据）和反馈数据（满意度、疲劳度、喜欢的艺术品等）

Result: 成功构建了开放数据集，能够重现文献中的参观者特征和Veron与Levasseur的参观者画像

Conclusion: 该数据集将用于改进博物馆推荐路径的质量，通过个性化兴趣点数量、停留时间和信息量来适应不同参观者的兴趣水平

Abstract: Lack of data is a recurring problem in Artificial Intelligence, as it is essential for training and validating models. This is particularly true in the field of cultural heritage, where the number of open datasets is relatively limited and where the data collected does not always allow for holistic modeling of visitors' experience due to the fact that data are ad hoc (i.e. restricted to the sole characteristics required for the evaluation of a specific model). To overcome this lack, we conducted a study between February and March 2019 aimed at obtaining comprehensive and detailed information about visitors, their visit experience and their feedback. We equipped 51 participants with eye-tracking glasses, leaving them free to explore the 3 floors of the museum for an average of 57 minutes, and to discover an exhibition of more than 400 artworks. On this basis, we built an open dataset combining contextual data (demographic data, preferences, visiting habits, motivations, social context. . . ), behavioral data (spatiotemporal trajectories, gaze data) and feedback (satisfaction, fatigue, liked artworks, verbatim. . . ). Our analysis made it possible to re-enact visitor identities combining the majority of characteristics found in the literature and to reproduce the Veron and Levasseur profiles. This dataset will ultimately make it possible to improve the quality of recommended paths in museums by personalizing the number of points of interest (POIs), the time spent at these different POIs, and the amount of information to be provided to each visitor based on their level of interest.

</details>


### [3] [DiSCoKit: An Open-Source Toolkit for Deploying Live LLM Experiences in Survey Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11230)
*Jaime Banks,Jon Stromer-Galley,Samiksha Singh,Collin Capano*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: DiSCoKit是一个开源工具包，用于在JavaScript支持的在线调查平台（如Qualtrics）上部署实时大型语言模型体验，解决人机交互研究中AI刺激部署的技术挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机交互的社会科学研究需要向参与者提供实时LLM体验，但技术挑战（从聊天数据记录到AI行为操控）阻碍了基于调查的AI刺激部署。

Method: 开发了DiSCoKit开源工具包，支持通过JavaScript调查平台部署基于Microsoft Azure门户等模型的实时LLM体验，包含科学动机、架构设计和操作说明。

Result: 提出了一个功能完整的工具包，能够处理聊天数据记录、AI行为操控等关键技术挑战，支持在Qualtrics等平台上部署实验性AI刺激。

Conclusion: DiSCoKit为人机交互研究提供了实用的技术解决方案，降低了在线调查中部署实时AI体验的门槛，但存在部署可能性和局限性需要进一步考虑。

Abstract: Advancing social-scientific research of human-AI interaction dynamics and outcomes often requires researchers to deliver experiences with live large-language models (LLMs) to participants through online survey platforms. However, technical and practical challenges (from logging chat data to manipulating AI behaviors for experimental designs) often inhibit survey-based deployment of AI stimuli. We developed DiSCoKit--an open-source toolkit for deploying live LLM experiences (e.g., ones based on models delivered through Microsoft Azure portal) through JavaScript-enabled survey platforms (e.g., Qualtrics). This paper introduces that toolkit, explaining its scientific impetus, describes its architecture and operation, as well as its deployment possibilities and limitations.

</details>


### [4] [Same Feedback, Different Source: How AI vs. Human Feedback Shapes Learner Engagement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11311)
*Caitlin Morris,Pattie Maes*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨了学习者对反馈来源的认知如何影响其参与度，通过实验发现即使反馈内容相同，被标记为人类助教的反馈比标记为AI的反馈能引发更多时间和精力投入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI与人类教师共同参与教育过程，理解学习者对反馈来源的归因效应对于设计有效的混合AI-人类教育系统至关重要。需要探究学习者对AI与人类反馈的不同认知如何影响其学习参与度和反馈评价。

Method: 设计了一个创意编程界面，在控制反馈内容完全相同的条件下，将参与者分为两组：一组被告知反馈来自AI，另一组被告知反馈来自人类助教。所有反馈实际上都由LLM生成，仅来源标签不同。通过测量参与者的时间投入、努力程度以及对反馈的评价来比较两组差异。

Result: 1. 反馈来源显著影响参与度：人类助教组的参与者在相同反馈上花费的时间和精力显著更多（效应量d = 0.88-1.56）。2. 评价机制不同：AI反馈的评价主要受先前对AI信任度影响（r = 0.85），而人类助教反馈的评价则受感知真诚度影响（r = 0.65）。

Conclusion: 反馈来源不仅影响学习者对反馈的评价方式，还显著影响其参与程度。这对混合教育系统设计有重要启示：需要考虑如何呈现反馈来源以优化学习效果，可能需要策略性地管理学习者对反馈来源的认知。

Abstract: When learners receive feedback, what they believe about its source may shape how they engage with it. As AI is used alongside human instructors, understanding these attribution effects is essential for designing effective hybrid AI-human educational systems. We designed a creative coding interface that isolates source attribution while controlling for content: all participants receive identical LLM-generated feedback, but half see it attributed to AI and half to a human teaching assistant (TA). We found two key results. First, perceived feedback source affected engagement: learners in the TA condition spent significantly more time and effort (d = 0.88-1.56) despite receiving identical feedback. Second, perceptions differed: AI-attributed feedback ratings were predicted by prior trust in AI (r = 0.85), while TA-attributed ratings were predicted by perceived genuineness (r = 0.65). These findings suggest that feedback source shapes both engagement and evaluation, with implications for hybrid educational system design.

</details>


### [5] [Mapping the Landscape of Affective Extended Reality: A Scoping Review of Biodata-Driven Systems for Understanding and Sharing Emotions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11710)
*Zhidian Lin,Allison Jing,Ziyuan Qu,Fabio Zambetta,Ryan M. Kelly*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 对82篇论文进行范围综述，分析生物数据、情感与XR交叉领域的研究现状，提出"情感扩展现实"概念并绘制该领域全景图


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HCI文献中包含许多利用生物数据理解情感XR系统，但这些系统尚未被系统性地整合和映射，缺乏对该领域的整体理解

Method: 对82篇论文进行范围综述，分析这些系统中使用的技术、交互技术以及评估方法，绘制情感XR领域现状图

Result: 揭示了情感共享目标的多样性，展示了HCI研究者如何探索XR生物反馈系统的交互流程设计，突出了关键设计维度和理解情感的挑战

Conclusion: 识别了情感共享中未充分利用的方法，强调了情感XR未来研究的机会，为该领域提供了系统性框架和方向指引

Abstract: This paper introduces the notion of affective extended reality (XR) to characterise XR systems that use biodata to enable understanding of emotions. The HCI literature contains many such systems, but they have not yet been mapped into a coherent whole. To address this, we conducted a scoping review of 82 papers that explore the nexus of biodata, emotions, and XR. We analyse the technologies used in these systems, the interaction techniques employed, and the methods used to evaluate their effectiveness. Through our analysis, we contribute a mapping of the current landscape of affective XR, revealing diversity in the goals for enabling emotion sharing. We demonstrate how HCI researchers have explored the design of the interaction flows in XR biofeedback systems, highlighting key design dimensions and challenges in understanding emotions. We discuss underused approaches for emotion sharing and highlight opportunities for future research on affective XR.

</details>


### [6] [Situated, Dynamic, and Subjective: Envisioning the Design of Theory-of-Mind-Enabled Everyday AI with Industry Practitioners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11342)
*Qiaosi Wang,Jini Kim,Avanita Sharma,Alicia,Lee,Jodi Forlizzi,Hong Shen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究通过13场与AI从业者的协同设计会议，提出了心理理论（ToM）赋能AI产品的三项设计建议，并揭示了理想愿景与现实实践之间的设计张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然心理理论（ToM）被认为是人类社交互动的核心能力，且AI领域的ToM能力构建和基准测试日益增多，但关于这种能力如何转化为日常用户AI产品的设计和体验却知之甚少。研究旨在探索ToM赋能AI产品在实际设计开发中的可能性。

Method: 研究采用协同设计方法，与26名美国AI从业者进行了13场设计会议，通过共同构思、反思和提炼，探索既具前瞻性又扎根于AI设计开发实践现实的ToM赋能日常AI产品设计建议。

Result: 分析揭示了三项相互关联的设计建议：1）ToM赋能AI应置于塑造用户心理状态的社会情境中；2）应对心理状态的动态性做出响应；3）应适应主观个体差异。每项建议内部都存在设计张力，揭示了从业者对ToM赋能AI的未来愿景与当前AI设计开发实践现实之间的差距。

Conclusion: 研究指出需要超越静态的、推理驱动的ToM方法，转向将ToM设计为支持持续人机交互循环的普适能力，以弥合理想愿景与现实实践之间的差距。

Abstract: Theory of Mind (ToM) -- the ability to infer what others are thinking (e.g., intentions) from observable cues -- is traditionally considered fundamental to human social interactions. This has sparked growing efforts in building and benchmarking AI's ToM capability, yet little is known about how such capability could translate into the design and experience of everyday user-facing AI products and services. We conducted 13 co-design sessions with 26 U.S.-based AI practitioners to envision, reflect, and distill design recommendations for ToM-enabled everyday AI products and services that are both future-looking and grounded in the realities of AI design and development practices. Analysis revealed three interrelated design recommendations: ToM-enabled AI should 1) be situated in the social context that shape users' mental states, 2) be responsive to the dynamic nature of mental states, and 3) be attuned to subjective individual differences. We surface design tensions within each recommendation that reveal a broader gap between practitioners' envisioned futures of ToM-enabled AI and the realities of current AI design and development practices. These findings point toward the need to move beyond static, inference-driven approach to ToM and toward designing ToM as a pervasive capability that supports continuous human-AI interaction loops.

</details>


### [7] [Interpretive Cultures: Resonance, randomness, and negotiated meaning for AI-assisted tarot divination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11367)
*Matthew Prock,Ziv Epstein,Hope Schroeder,Amy Smith,Cassandra Lee,Vana Goblot,Farnaz Jahanbakhsh*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨AI在塔罗牌占卜等主观解释性实践中的应用，分析用户如何通过AI寻求共鸣解释，并提出支持解释性意义建构的AI设计建议


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在创意和分析任务中应用广泛，但在主观、多元、非因果的解释性实践中的作用尚不明确。本研究旨在探索AI辅助塔罗牌占卜这一占卜实践中，用户如何寻求、协商和评估共鸣解释

Method: 基于对塔罗牌实践者的访谈，运用Hartmut Rosa的共鸣理论，研究用户在查询与解释数据之间不存在因果关系的情境下，如何整合AI到解释性工作流程中

Result: 识别出实践者将AI融入解释工作流程的多种方式：利用AI应对不确定性和自我怀疑、探索替代视角、简化或扩展现有占卜实践。发现用户寻求的是与个人体验产生共鸣的解释，而非因果解释

Conclusion: 提出支持解释性意义建构的AI系统设计建议，强调系统应保持模糊性、不剥夺用户能动性，能够支持主观解释过程而不将其简化为因果推理

Abstract: While generative AI tools are increasingly adopted for creative and analytical tasks, their role in interpretive practices, where meaning is subjective, plural, and non-causal, remains poorly understood. This paper examines AI-assisted tarot reading, a divinatory practice in which users pose a query, draw cards through a randomized process, and ask AI systems to interpret the resulting symbols. Drawing on interviews with tarot practitioners and Hartmut Rosa's Theory of Resonance, we investigate how users seek, negotiate, and evaluate resonant interpretations in a context where no causal relationship exists between the query and the data being interpreted. We identify distinct ways practitioners incorporate AI into their interpretive workflows, including using AI to navigate uncertainty and self-doubt, explore alternative perspectives, and streamline or extend existing divinatory practices. Based on these findings, we offer design recommendations for AI systems that support interpretive meaning-making without collapsing ambiguity or foreclosing user agency.

</details>


### [8] [Data-driven modelling of low-dimensional dynamical structures underlying complex full-body human movement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11492)
*Ryota Takamido,Chiharu Suzuki,Hiroki Nakamoto*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 使用神经常微分方程（NODEs）建模非周期性全身运动的低维潜在动力学系统，以扩展动力学系统方法（DSA）到更复杂、生态效度更高的人类运动


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学系统方法（DSA）在解决人类运动控制的自由度问题方面提供了有价值的见解，但其应用主要局限于周期性或简化的运动。需要将DSA扩展到更复杂、生态效度更高的非周期性全身运动。

Method: 采用神经常微分方程（NODEs）建模非周期性全身运动的时间演化过程，将其表示为低维潜在动力学系统。选择棒球投球作为代表性目标运动，验证DSA在复杂运动中的应用。

Result: 验证实验表明，基于NODE的动力学模型能够准确预测复杂投球动作的时间演化（R^2 > 0.45）。特别值得注意的是，仅使用前约8%的时间序列就能解释投球动作后半段约50%的方差，表明后续运动如何从初始条件根据潜在空间中ODE定义的动力学演化而来。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，动力学系统方法（DSA）有潜力扩展到更复杂、生态效度更高的人类运动形式，为解决人类运动控制的自由度问题提供了新的建模框架。

Abstract: One of the central challenges in the study of human motor control and learning is the degrees-of-freedom problem. Although the dynamical systems approach (DSA) has provided valuable insights into addressing this issue, its application has largely been confined to cyclic or simplified motor movements. To overcome this limitation, the present study employs neural ordinary differential equations (NODEs) to model the time evolution of non-cyclic full-body movements as a low-dimensional latent dynamical system. Given the temporal complexity full-body kinematic chains, baseball pitching was selected as a representative target movement to examine whether DSA could be extended to more complex, ecologically valid human movements. Results of the verification experiment demonstrated that the time evolution of a complex pitching motion could be accurately predicted (R^2 > 0.45) using the NODE-based dynamical model. Notably, approximately 50% of the variance in the latter half of the pitching motion was explained using only the initial ~8% of the temporal sequence, underscoring how subsequent movement evolves from initial conditions according to ODE-defined dynamics in latent space. These findings indicate the potential to extend the DSA to more complex and ecologically valid forms of human movement.

</details>


### [9] [An Educational Human Machine Interface Providing Request-to-Intervene Trigger and Reason Explanation for Enhancing the Driver's Comprehension of ADS's System Limitations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11507)
*Ryuji Matsuo,Hailong Liu,Toshihiro Hiraoka,Takahiro Wada*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出基于语音的教育性人机界面，通过提供接管请求触发线索和原因，帮助驾驶员理解自动驾驶系统限制，提高接管安全性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: L3级自动驾驶系统在超出运行设计域时会发出接管请求，但复杂交通场景中驾驶员可能同时感知多个潜在触发因素，导致接管时犹豫或困惑。驾驶员需要清晰理解系统限制以确保安全接管。

Method: 提出基于语音的教育性人机界面，在接管请求中提供触发线索和原因。通过驾驶模拟器进行组间实验，比较不同人机界面条件对驾驶员理解系统限制和接管行为的影响。

Result: 实验结果显示，在接管请求中加入有效的触发线索和原因与驾驶员对系统限制的理解提升相关。大多数使用该方法的参与者能在接管请求失败时主动接管控制，且碰撞次数低于其他人机界面条件。

Conclusion: 通过所提方法持续增强驾驶员对自动驾驶系统限制的理解，与更安全有效的实时交互相关。基于语音的教育性人机界面有助于提高L3级自动驾驶系统的接管安全性。

Abstract: Level 3 automated driving systems (ADS) have attracted significant attention and are being commercialized. A level 3 ADS prompts the driver to take control by issuing a request to intervene (RtI) when its operational design domains (ODD) are exceeded. However, complex traffic situations can cause drivers to perceive multiple potential triggers of RtI simultaneously, causing hesitation or confusion during take-over. Therefore, drivers need to clearly understand the ADS's system limitations to ensure safe take-over. This study proposes a voice-based educational human machine interface~(HMI) for providing RtI trigger cues and reason to help drivers understand ADS's system limitations. The results of a between-group experiment using a driving simulator showed that incorporating effective trigger cues and reason into the RtI was related to improved driver comprehension of the ADS's system limitations. Moreover, most participants, instructed via the proposed method, could proactively take over control of the ADS in cases where RtI fails; meanwhile, their number of collisions was lower compared with the other RtI HMI conditions. Therefore, using the proposed method to continually enhance the driver's understanding of the system limitations of ADS through the proposed method is associated with safer and more effective real-time interactions with ADS.

</details>


### [10] [Implications of AI Involvement for Trust in Expert Advisory Workflows Under Epistemic Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11522)
*Dennis Kim,Roya Daneshi,Bruce Draper,Sarath Sreedharan*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究AI助手如何影响用户对人类专家、AI系统及其组合的信任，通过课程规划任务实验发现专家使用AI的方式会影响用户对其专业性和信任度的感知


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI工具在医疗、法律、金融等专业工作流程中的日益整合，需要了解AI参与如何影响用户对人类专家、AI系统及其组合的信任

Method: 进行用户研究（N=77），使用模拟课程规划任务，比较不同条件下AI的存在和人类-AI协作的具体模式

Result: 虽然顾问制定正确时间表的能力很重要，但用户对专业性和信任的感知也受到专家如何利用AI助手的影响

Conclusion: 这些发现对人类-AI混合团队的设计具有重要意义，特别是在采纳建议取决于最终用户对推荐者专业性的感知时

Abstract: The increasing integration of AI-powered tools into expert workflows, such as medicine, law, and finance, raises a critical question: how does AI involvement influence a user's trust in the human expert, the AI system, and their combination? To investigate this, we conducted a user study (N=77) featuring a simulated course-planning task. We compared various conditions that differed in both the presence of AI and the specific mode of human-AI collaboration. Our results indicate that while the advisor's ability to create a correct schedule is important, the user's perception of expertise and trust is also influenced by how the expert utilized the AI assistant. These findings raise important considerations for the design of human-AI hybrid teams, particularly when the adoption of recommendations depends on the end-user's perception of the recommender's expertise.

</details>


### [11] [Behavioral Indicators of Overreliance During Interaction with Conversational Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11567)
*Chang Liu,Qinyi Zhou,Xinjie Shen,Xingyu Bruce Liu,Tongshuang Wu,Xiang 'Anthony' Chen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析用户与注入错误信息的LLM交互行为，识别出与过度依赖相关的五种行为模式，为缓解过度依赖提供设计启示


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在广泛日常场景中应用，但其固有的幻觉风险可能导致流畅响应中隐藏错误信息，引发对AI过度依赖的担忧。检测过度依赖具有挑战性，因为它常出现在复杂动态情境中，难以通过事后任务结果简单捕捉

Method: 收集77名参与者在三个真实世界任务中与注入合理错误信息的LLM的交互日志，通过参与者是否检测并纠正这些错误来评估过度依赖程度。通过语义编码和聚类用户交互片段，识别与过度依赖相关的行为模式

Result: 识别出五种与过度依赖相关的行为模式：低过度依赖用户表现出仔细的任务理解和细粒度导航；高过度依赖用户表现出频繁复制粘贴、跳过初始理解、重复参考LLM、粗略定位以及尽管犹豫仍接受错误信息

Conclusion: 研究揭示了用户与LLM交互中与过度依赖相关的具体行为模式，为设计缓解过度依赖的干预措施提供了实证基础

Abstract: LLMs are now embedded in a wide range of everyday scenarios. However, their inherent hallucinations risk hiding misinformation in fluent responses, raising concerns about overreliance on AI. Detecting overreliance is challenging, as it often arises in complex, dynamic contexts and cannot be easily captured by post-hoc task outcomes. In this work, we aim to investigate how users' behavioral patterns correlate with overreliance. We collected interaction logs from 77 participants working with an LLM injected plausible misinformation across three real-world tasks and we assessed overreliance by whether participants detected and corrected these errors. By semantically encoding and clustering segments of user interactions, we identified five behavioral patterns linked to overreliance: users with low overreliance show careful task comprehension and fine-grained navigation; users with high overreliance show frequent copy-paste, skipping initial comprehension, repeated LLM references, coarse locating, and accepting misinformation despite hesitation. We discuss design implications for mitigation.

</details>


### [12] ["I Was Told to Come Back and Share This": Social Media-Based Near-Death Experience Disclosures as Expressions of Spiritual Beliefs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11663)
*Yifan Zhao,Yuxin Fang,Yihuan Chen,RAY LC*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究分析了200个TikTok上#nde标签视频，发现濒死体验叙事常被用于表达个人意义，多数包含灵性与宗教主题，这些内容能吸引更多互动并促进关于信仰的深度对话。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然学者研究过个人如何分享濒死体验，但缺乏对当今社交媒体平台上这些叙事如何被协作传播的研究。本研究旨在探索濒死体验叙事在TikTok等社交媒体上的传播方式和社区互动模式。

Method: 采用内容分析方法，随机抽样分析了200个带有#nde及相关标签的TikTok视频，并对评论区进行了分析以了解用户互动模式。

Result: 研究发现：1）濒死体验叙事常被用于表达个人意义；2）大多数帖子包含灵性与宗教主题；3）包含灵性主题的视频能吸引更多用户参与；4）这些视频促进了关于信仰和意义的深度对话。

Conclusion: 在线平台促进了社区层面的灵性参与，研究结果为设计支持特殊社区共享表达和连接的空间提供了启示，强调了社交媒体在促进灵性对话和社区建设中的作用。

Abstract: People who experienced near-death events often turn to personal expression as a way of processing trauma and articulating beliefs. While scholars have examined how individuals share near-death experiences (NDEs), limited research has explored how these narratives are communicated collaboratively on today's social media platforms. We analyzed 200 randomly sampled TikTok videos tagged with #nde and related hashtags. Content analysis revealed that individuals often use NDE narratives to articulate personal meaning, with spiritual and religious themes appearing in the majority of posts and serving as a means of exploring and making sense of personal spiritual perspectives. Consistent with this, analyses of comment sections reveal that videos containing spiritual themes tend to attract more engagement and foster deeper conversations around faith and meaning. Our findings offer insights into how online platforms facilitate community-level engagement with spirituality, and suggest implications for design of spaces that support shared expression and connection in specialized communities.

</details>


### [13] [Building Intelligent User Interfaces for Human-AI Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11753)
*Danqing Shi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一个用于分析用户界面如何改进人机对齐的参考模型，通过案例研究和初步调查展示其应用价值


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI对齐研究主要关注训练算法，而用户界面常被忽视或仅视为实现细节，而非对齐的关键因素。本文旨在填补这一空白，强调用户界面在人机对齐中的重要作用。

Method: 引入一个参考模型，提供系统化框架来分析用户界面在哪些方面以及如何改进人机对齐。通过两个案例研究和包含六个用户界面的初步调查来演示该结构化分类法的应用。

Result: 该参考模型提供了一个结构化分类法，能够系统地分析用户界面对人机对齐的贡献。案例研究和初步调查展示了该模型的实际应用价值。

Conclusion: 用户界面在人机对齐中扮演关键角色，不应仅被视为实现细节。该参考模型为通过人机交互推进对齐研究提供了系统化框架和机会。

Abstract: Aligning AI systems with human values fundamentally relies on effective human feedback. While significant research has addressed training algorithms, the role of user interface is often overlooked and only treated as an implementation detail rather than a critical factor of alignment. This paper addresses this gap by introducing a reference model that offers a systematic framework for analyzing where and how user interface contributions can improve human-AI alignment. The structured taxonomy of the reference model is demonstrated through two case studies and a preliminary investigation featuring six user interfaces. This work highlights opportunities to advance alignment through human-computer interaction.

</details>


### [14] [V-SHiNE: A Virtual Smart Home Framework for Explainability Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11775)
*Mersedeh Sadeghi,Simon Scholz,Max Unterbusch,Andreas Vogelsang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: V-SHiNE是一个基于浏览器的智能家居仿真框架，用于可扩展、现实地评估解释系统，解决了该领域方法学上的困难


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释对于帮助用户理解和信任自主智能家居决策至关重要，但在该领域评估解释质量和影响仍存在方法学上的困难

Method: 开发了基于浏览器的智能家居仿真框架V-SHiNE，允许研究人员配置环境、模拟行为、插入自定义解释引擎，提供灵活的交付模式和丰富的交互日志记录

Result: 159名参与者的研究证明了该框架的可行性，V-SHiNE为推进以用户为中心的可解释智能系统评估提供了轻量级、可复现的平台

Conclusion: V-SHiNE填补了智能家居领域解释评估的方法学空白，为研究人员提供了一个可扩展、现实且可复现的评估平台

Abstract: Explanations are essential for helping users interpret and trust autonomous smart-home decisions, yet evaluating their quality and impact remains methodologically difficult in this domain. V-SHiNE addresses this gap: a browser-based smarthome simulation framework for scalable and realistic assessment of explanations. It allows researchers to configure environments, simulate behaviors, and plug in custom explanation engines, with flexible delivery modes and rich interaction logging. A study with 159 participants demonstrates its feasibility. V-SHiNE provides a lightweight, reproducible platform for advancing user-centered evaluation of explainable intelligent systems

</details>


### [15] [Decision Support System for Technology Opportunity Discovery: An Application of the Schwartz Theory of Basic Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11855)
*Ayato Kitadai,Takumi Ito,Yumiko Nagoh,Hiroki Takahashi,Masanori Fujita,Sangjic Lee,Fumiaki Miyahara,Tetsu Natsume,Nariaki Nishino*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一个结合技术就绪度(TRL)和人类基本价值理论(Schwartz)的决策支持框架，用于早期技术机会发现，通过案例研究验证了其可行性和洞察潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技术机会发现方法在早期开发阶段往往未能系统整合终端用户视角，导致技术潜力与市场相关性错位，特别是在消费者需求不明确的情况下。

Method: 提出一个新颖的决策支持框架，整合两个不同视角：基于工程的技术就绪度(TRL)和Schwartz的人类基本价值理论。通过探索性研讨会，分析索尼计算机科学实验室的四个真实技术案例（两个商业成功和两个失败）。

Result: 发现两个一致模式：1)内部专家比消费者识别出更广泛的价值景观（愿景差距）；2)成功技术展现出更广泛的相关人类价值（价值广度），表明战略远见可能支撑市场成功。

Conclusion: 本研究为早期研发决策提供了实用工具，并建立了价值理论与创新成果之间的理论联系。虽然探索性有限，但结果突显了以价值为中心评估作为更以人为本的技术机会发现基础的潜力。

Abstract: Discovering technology opportunities (TOD) remains a critical challenge for innovation management, especially in early-stage development where consumer needs are often unclear. Existing methods frequently fail to systematically incorporate end-user perspectives, resulting in a misalignment between technological potentials and market relevance. This study proposes a novel decision support framework that bridges this gap by linking technological feasibility with fundamental human values. The framework integrates two distinct lenses: the engineering-based Technology Readiness Levels (TRL) and Schwartz's theory of basic human values. By combining these, the approach enables a structured exploration of how emerging technologies may satisfy diverse user motivations. To illustrate the framework's feasibility and insight potential, we conducted exploratory workshops with general consumers and internal experts at Sony Computer Science Laboratories, Inc., analyzing four real-world technologies (two commercial successes and two failures). Two consistent patterns emerged: (1) internal experts identified a wider value landscape than consumers (vision gap), and (2) successful technologies exhibited a broader range of associated human values (value breadth), suggesting strategic foresight may underpin market success. This study contributes both a practical tool for early-stage R\&D decision-making and a theoretical link between value theory and innovation outcomes. While exploratory in scope, the findings highlight the promise of value-centric evaluation as a foundation for more human-centered technology opportunity discovery.

</details>


### [16] [Who Does What? Archetypes of Roles Assigned to LLMs During Human-AI Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11924)
*Shreya Chappidi,Jatinder Singh,Andra V. Krauze*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文提出人类-LLM原型概念，通过文献分析识别出17种人机协作决策模式，并在临床诊断案例中评估不同原型对LLM输出和决策结果的影响，探讨了设计权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在高风险决策领域应用增加，需要批判性反思影响人类与LLM角色分配和交互的社会技术因素，理解不同人机协作模式对决策结果的影响。

Method: 通过范围性文献综述和主题分析113篇LLM支持决策的论文，识别出17种人类-LLM原型；在真实临床诊断案例中评估不同原型对LLM输出和决策结果的影响。

Result: 识别出17种人类-LLM决策协作原型；发现原型选择会影响LLM输出和决策结果；揭示了决策控制、社会层级、认知强制策略和信息需求等关键设计权衡。

Conclusion: 人类-LLM交互原型的选择对LLM输出和决策有显著影响，为人类-AI决策系统设计者提供了重要的风险考量框架和设计选择指导。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly supporting decision-making across high-stakes domains, requiring critical reflection on the socio-technical factors that shape how humans and LLMs are assigned roles and interact during human-in-the-loop decision-making. This paper introduces the concept of human-LLM archetypes -- defined as re-curring socio-technical interaction patterns that structure the roles of humans and LLMs in collaborative decision-making. We describe 17 human-LLM archetypes derived from a scoping literature review and thematic analysis of 113 LLM-supported decision-making papers. Then, we evaluate these diverse archetypes across real-world clinical diagnostic cases to examine the potential effects of adopting distinct human-LLM archetypes on LLM outputs and decision outcomes. Finally, we present relevant tradeoffs and design choices across human-LLM archetypes, including decision control, social hierarchies, cognitive forcing strategies, and information requirements. Through our analysis, we show that selection of human-LLM interaction archetype can influence LLM outputs and decisions, bringing important risks and considerations for the designers of human-AI decision-making systems

</details>


### [17] [Wisdom of the LLM Crowd: A Large Scale Benchmark of Multi-Label U.S. Election-Related Harmful Social Media Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11962)
*Qile Wang,Prerana Khatiwada,Carolina Coimbra Vieira,Benjamin E. Bagozzi,Kenneth E. Barner,Matthew Louis Mauriello*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: USE24-XD是一个包含近10万条推文的大规模数据集，收集自2024年美国总统选举周期，使用6个LLM对阴谋论、煽情主义、仇恨言论、猜测和讽刺等5个类别进行标注，并通过众包验证，旨在支持有害政治内容的早期检测研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选举错误信息和有害政治内容的传播威胁民主完整性，早期检测对于理解和缓解其传播至关重要。需要大规模、低成本的数据集来支持相关研究。

Method: 收集近10万条X平台推文，使用6个大型语言模型对5个类别（阴谋论、煽情主义、仇恨言论、猜测、讽刺）进行系统标注，通过众包验证LLM标注质量，采用群体智慧方法聚合LLM标注，分析人类标注者人口统计学特征对标注行为的影响。

Result: LLM与人类标注者具有可比性的一致性模式，LLM表现出更高的内部一致性，在猜测类别上达到0.90召回率。60%的帖子至少获得一个标签。人类标注者的政治意识形态和党派归属显著影响标注行为，揭示了有害内容判断中的主观性来源。

Conclusion: LLM能够以低成本实现大规模有害内容标注，与人类标注质量相当。USE24-XD数据集为研究有害政治内容的早期检测提供了宝贵资源，同时揭示了内容标注中的主观性因素，强调了考虑标注者背景的重要性。

Abstract: The spread of election misinformation and harmful political content conveys misleading narratives and poses a serious threat to democratic integrity. Detecting harmful content at early stages is essential for understanding and potentially mitigating its downstream spread. In this study, we introduce USE24-XD, a large-scale dataset of nearly 100k posts collected from X (formerly Twitter) during the 2024 U.S. presidential election cycle, enriched with spatio-temporal metadata. To substantially reduce the cost of manual annotation while enabling scalable categorization, we employ six large language models (LLMs) to systematically annotate posts across five nuanced categories: Conspiracy, Sensationalism, Hate Speech, Speculation, and Satire. We validate LLM annotations with crowdsourcing (n = 34) and benchmark them against human annotators. Inter-rater reliability analyses show comparable agreement patterns between LLMs and humans, with LLMs exhibiting higher internal consistency and achieving up to 0.90 recall on Speculation. We apply a wisdom-of-the-crowd approach across LLMs to aggregate annotations and curate a robust multi-label dataset. 60% of posts receive at least one label. We further analyze how human annotator demographics, including political ideology and affiliation, shape labeling behavior, highlighting systematic sources of subjectivity in judgments of harmful content. The USE24-XD dataset is publicly released to support future research.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [18] [Multi Layer Protection Against Low Rate DDoS Attacks in Containerized Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11407)
*Ahmad Fareed,Bilal Al Habib,Anne Pepita Francis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一个针对容器化云环境中低速率DDoS攻击的多层防御系统，集成WAF、速率限制、动态黑名单、TCP/UDP头部分析和零信任原则


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低速率DDoS攻击对容器化云基础设施构成严重威胁，由于其流量小难以检测和缓解，可能对互联网应用造成严重损害

Method: 采用多层防御策略，集成Web应用防火墙(WAF)、速率限制、动态黑名单、TCP和UDP头部分析，并应用零信任原则，在攻击生命周期的不同阶段检测和阻止恶意流量

Result: 系统能够有效防御容器化环境中的低速率DDoS攻击，通过零信任原则确保每个数据包在授权访问前都经过仔细检查，提高整体安全性和弹性

Conclusion: 该解决方案与Docker编排集成，便于在容器化环境中部署和管理，为容器化云基础设施提供了有效的低速率DDoS攻击防御机制

Abstract: Low rate Distributed Denial of Service DDoS attacks have emerged as a major threat to containerized cloud infrastructures. Due to their low traffic volumes, these attacks can be difficult to detect and mitigate, potentially causing serious harm to internet applications. This work proposes a DDoS mitigation system that effectively defends against low rate DDoS attacks in containerized environments using a multi layered defense strategy. The solution integrates a Web Application Firewall WAF, rate limiting, dynamic blacklisting, TCP and UDP header analysis, and zero trust principles to detect and block malicious traffic at different stages of the attack life cycle. By applying zero trust principles, the system ensures that each data packet is carefully inspected before granting access, improving overall security and resilience. Additionally, the systems integration with Docker orchestration facilitates deployment and management in containerized settings.

</details>


### [19] [Modelling Trust and Trusted Systems: A Category Theoretic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11376)
*Ian Oliver,Pekka Kuure*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出基于范畴论的信任建模框架，用于可信计算系统和远程证明，通过范畴对象和态射形式化信任要素，使用Heyting代数实现多级信任决策，并利用指数运算定义证明操作的组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信任模型通常过于简化，缺乏对信任建立过程的严格形式化，特别是对于可信计算系统和远程证明环境。需要更精确的语义来定义"可信性"和"正当性/取证"等概念，并超越简单的二元信任状态。

Method: 采用范畴论框架：将元素、声明、结果和决策建模为范畴中的对象；将证明、验证和决策过程建模为态射。使用Heyting代数形式化信任决策空间，支持多级信任。利用范畴论中的指数运算定义证明操作的组合，并建立证明环境表达能力的基础度量。

Result: 开发了严格的信任建模框架，提供了信任建立的形式化理解。通过Heyting代数实现了超越二元状态的细致信任级别。展示了包括启动-运行-关闭序列、Evil Maid攻击和基于该模型的证明环境规范在内的多个工作示例。解决了多组合动态大系统的建模挑战。

Conclusion: 该范畴论框架为可信计算系统和远程证明提供了严格的信任建模方法，能够形式化复杂的信任关系，支持多级信任决策，并为证明操作的组合和表达能力度量提供了理论基础，适用于动态和大规模系统的建模。

Abstract: We introduces a category-theoretic framework for modelling trust as applied to trusted computation systems and remote attestation. By formalizing elements, claims, results, and decisions as objects within a category, and the processes of attestation, verification, and decision-making as morphisms, the framework provides a rigorous approach to understanding trust establishment and provides a well-defined semantics for terms such as `trustworthiness' and 'justification'/forensics. The trust decision space is formalized using a Heyting Algebra, allowing nuanced trust levels that extend beyond binary trusted/untrusted states. We then present additional structures and in particular utilise exponentiation in a category theoretic sense to define compositions of attestation operations and provide the basis of a measurement for the expressibility of an attestation environment. We present a number of worked examples including boot-run-shutdown sequences, Evil Maid attacks and the specification of an attestation environment based upon this model. We then address challenges in modelling dynamic and larger systems made of multiple compositions.

</details>


### [20] [Security Assessment of Intel TDX with support for Live Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11434)
*Kirk Swidowski,Daniel Moghimi,Josh Eads,Erdem Aktas,Jia Ma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Google与Intel合作对Intel TDX 1.5进行安全评估，发现一个可完全攻陷TD的漏洞和四个可泄露TDX模块机密内存的漏洞，强调机密计算需要纵深防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估Intel TDX 1.5版本的新功能（实时迁移和TD分区）的安全性，延续Google之前对TDX 1.0的审查工作，为机密计算提供更全面的安全评估。

Method: 与Intel合作获得TDX 1.5源代码和文档；使用支持TDX的计算节点开发实时测试工具包和PoC生成工具；集成Gemini进行分析，使用NotebookLM高效导航复杂规范。

Result: 发现一个高危漏洞（VMM可完全攻陷TD）和四个中危漏洞（恶意VMM或TD可泄露TDX模块机密内存）；识别了多个安全弱点/错误但未归类为漏洞。

Conclusion: 机密计算与其他安全措施一样需要迭代改进和互补的安全控制，应采用纵深防御方法；评估结果强调了持续安全审查的重要性。

Abstract: In the second and third quarters of 2025, Google collaborated with Intel to conduct a security assessment of Intel Trust Domain Extensions (TDX), extending Google's previous review and covering major changes since Intel TDX Module 1.0 - namely support for Live Migration and Trusted Domain (TD) Partitioning (nested VMs within TDs). Intel provided guidance and support, including documentation and updated TDX 1.5 source code. Unlike the previous review, this time, we had access to a compute node capable of running TDX to develop a toolkit for live testing and Proof-of-Concept (PoC) generation. Furthermore, we integrated Gemini for analysis and NotebookLM to efficiently navigate complex specifications.
  This assessment resulted in the discovery of one vulnerability that enables a VMM to fully compromise a TD, and four vulnerabilities that enable a malicious VMM or TD to leak confidential memory of the Intel TDX Module. Several other security weaknesses and/or bugs were identified but not categorized as vulnerabilities despite having some impact on security.
  Beyond presenting the technical details of multiple bugs and vulnerabilities in this report, these findings underscore that confidential computing, like other security measures, requires iterative refinement and complementary security controls to harden it, in line with a defense-in-depth approach.

</details>


### [21] [Hardening the OSv Unikernel with Efficient Address Randomization: Design and Performance Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11445)
*Alex Wollman,John Hastings*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 在OSv unikernel中实现ASLR式地址随机化，通过随机化应用程序基址和线程栈区域来减少内存布局可预测性，提升安全防御能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Unikernels通常省略ASLR等安全缓解措施，OSv中的引导、程序加载和线程创建选择确定性地址，导致跨实例布局相似，使攻击更可重复利用。需要减少布局可预测性以提高安全性。

Method: 在OSv中引入ASLR式多样性，通过修改核心内存管理和加载例程来随机化应用程序基址和线程栈区域。实现保持OSv轻量级设计目标，仅增加最小复杂度。

Result: 与未修改基线相比，具有可比较的引导时间、应用程序运行时间和内存使用。生成的地址呈现均匀分布。实现有效提升了防御可靠利用的能力。

Conclusion: 布局随机化防御可以高效有效地集成到OSv unikernels中，提高抵抗可靠利用的能力，同时保持系统性能特征。

Abstract: Unikernels are single-purpose library operating systems that run the kernel and application in one address space, but often omit security mitigations such as address space layout randomization (ASLR). In OSv, boot, program loading, and thread creation select largely deterministic addresses, leading to near-identical layouts across instances and more repeatable exploitation. To reduce layout predictability, this research introduces ASLR-style diversity into OSv by randomizing the application base and thread stack regions through targeted changes to core memory-management and loading routines. The implementation adds minimal complexity while preserving OSv's lightweight design goals. Evaluation against an unmodified baseline finds comparable boot time, application runtime, and memory usage. Analysis indicates that the generated addresses exhibit a uniform distribution. These results show that layout-randomization defenses can be efficiently and effectively integrated into OSv unikernels, improving resistance to reliable exploitation.

</details>


### [22] [Cachemir: Fully Homomorphic Encrypted Inference of Generative Large Language Model with KV Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11470)
*Ye Yu,Yifan Zhou,Yi Chen,Pedro Soto,Wenjie Xiong,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Cachemir是一个基于全同态加密的KV缓存加速LLM推理框架，解决了现有FHE框架无法有效集成KV缓存导致自回归解码延迟过高的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM的广泛部署，用户将各种数据输入模型引发了严重的隐私担忧，需要基于全同态加密的安全推理框架。现有FHE框架的主要限制是无法有效集成KV缓存，导致自回归解码延迟过高。

Method: Cachemir包含三个关键技术贡献：1) 专门设计用于利用KV缓存计算优势的新型HE打包算法；2) 交错复制打包算法，用于高效计算Transformer线性层中KV缓存产生的向量-矩阵乘法；3) 考虑KV缓存的增强自举放置策略，以最小化自举成本。

Result: Cachemir在CPU上比MOAI (ICML'25)快48.83倍，比THOR (CCS'25)快67.16倍，在GPU上为Llama-3-8B生成一个输出token耗时不到100秒。

Conclusion: Cachemir成功解决了FHE框架中KV缓存集成的问题，显著提高了全同态加密LLM推理的效率，为隐私保护的LLM部署提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Generative large language models (LLMs) have revolutionized multiple domains. Modern LLMs predominantly rely on an autoregressive decoding strategy, which generates output tokens sequentially and employs a key-value cache (KV cache) to avoid redundant computation. However, the widespread deployment of LLMs has raised serious privacy concerns, as users are feeding all types of data into the model, motivating the development of secure inference frameworks based on fully homomorphic encryption (FHE). A major limitation of existing FHE-based frameworks is their inability to effectively integrate the KV cache, resulting in prohibitively high latency for autoregressive decoding. In this paper, we propose Cachemir, a KV Cache Accelerated Homomorphic Encrypted LLM Inference Regime to overcome this limitation. Cachemir comprises three key technical contributions: 1) a set of novel HE packing algorithms specifically designed to leverage the computational advantages of the KV cache; 2) an interleaved replicated packing algorithm to efficiently compute the vector-matrix multiplications that result from using the KV cache in Transformer linear layers; and 3) an augmented bootstrapping placement strategy that accounts for the KV cache to minimize bootstrapping cost. We demonstrate that Cachemir achieves $48.83\times$ and $67.16\times$ speedup over MOAI (ICML'25) and THOR (CCS'25) respectively on CPU and consumes less than 100 seconds on GPU to generate an output token for Llama-3-8B.

</details>


### [23] [Jailbreaking Leaves a Trace: Understanding and Detecting Jailbreak Attacks from Internal Representations of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11495)
*Sri Durga Sai Sowmya Kadali,Evangelos E. Papalexakis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于内部表征分析的LLM越狱检测与防御方法，通过分析隐藏层激活的潜在空间模式来识别和阻断越狱攻击，无需模型微调或额外检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着对话AI系统的广泛部署，LLM越狱已成为关键安全挑战。现有防御机制主要依赖提示层面的检测，但攻击者不断开发自适应策略，现有模型仍然脆弱。这促使需要从内部行为分析而非仅依赖提示层面防御的方法。

Method: 1) 对GPT-J、LLaMA、Mistral和Mamba等多个开源模型进行系统性的分层分析，识别越狱与良性提示的内部表征差异；2) 提出基于张量的潜在表征框架，捕捉隐藏层激活的结构特征；3) 利用潜在信号在推理时主动干扰越狱执行，通过选择性绕过高易感性层来阻断攻击。

Result: 在LLaMA-3.1-8B模型上，选择性绕过高易感性层可阻断78%的越狱尝试，同时对94%的良性提示保持正常行为。该方法完全在推理时运行，引入最小开销，为通过纳入更多攻击分布或更精细阈值实现更强覆盖提供了可扩展基础。

Conclusion: 越狱行为根植于可识别的内部结构，基于内部表征分析的方法为改进LLM安全提供了一种互补的、架构无关的方向。该方法无需模型微调或额外LLM检测器，具有轻量级和可扩展性优势。

Abstract: Jailbreaking large language models (LLMs) has emerged as a critical security challenge with the widespread deployment of conversational AI systems. Adversarial users exploit these models through carefully crafted prompts to elicit restricted or unsafe outputs, a phenomenon commonly referred to as Jailbreaking. Despite numerous proposed defense mechanisms, attackers continue to develop adaptive prompting strategies, and existing models remain vulnerable. This motivates approaches that examine the internal behavior of LLMs rather than relying solely on prompt-level defenses. In this work, we study jailbreaking from both security and interpretability perspectives by analyzing how internal representations differ between jailbreak and benign prompts. We conduct a systematic layer-wise analysis across multiple open-source models, including GPT-J, LLaMA, Mistral, and the state-space model Mamba, and identify consistent latent-space patterns associated with harmful inputs. We then propose a tensor-based latent representation framework that captures structure in hidden activations and enables lightweight jailbreak detection without model fine-tuning or auxiliary LLM-based detectors. We further demonstrate that the latent signals can be used to actively disrupt jailbreak execution at inference time. On an abliterated LLaMA-3.1-8B model, selectively bypassing high-susceptibility layers blocks 78% of jailbreak attempts while preserving benign behavior on 94% of benign prompts. This intervention operates entirely at inference time and introduces minimal overhead, providing a scalable foundation for achieving stronger coverage by incorporating additional attack distributions or more refined susceptibility thresholds. Our results provide evidence that jailbreak behavior is rooted in identifiable internal structures and suggest a complementary, architecture-agnostic direction for improving LLM security.

</details>


### [24] [Differentially Private and Communication Efficient Large Language Model Split Inference via Stochastic Quantization and Soft Prompt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11513)
*Yujie Gu,Richeng Jin,Xiaoyu Ji,Yier Jin,Wenyuan Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DEL框架：通过差分隐私和通信高效的LLM分割推理，在保护隐私的同时减少通信开销，无需本地模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM推理范式需要用户将查询发送给服务提供商处理，存在隐私泄露风险。现有方法虽然允许用户混淆词嵌入后再传输，但传输词嵌入和部署本地模型会导致过高的通信和计算开销，难以实际应用。

Method: 提出DEL框架：1) 嵌入投影模块和差分隐私随机量化机制，以隐私保护方式减少通信开销；2) 在服务器端适配软提示，补偿因隐私保护导致的效用下降，无需本地模型。

Result: 在文本生成和自然语言理解基准测试上的广泛实验证明了该方法的有效性。这是首个利用软提示来改善LLM推理中隐私与效用权衡的工作。

Conclusion: DEL框架通过差分隐私和通信高效的LLM分割推理，在保护用户隐私的同时显著减少了通信开销，并通过服务器端软提示补偿了效用损失，实现了隐私保护与实际部署的平衡。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable performance and received significant research interest. The enormous computational demands, however, hinder the local deployment on devices with limited resources. The current prevalent LLM inference paradigms require users to send queries to the service providers for processing, which raises critical privacy concerns. Existing approaches propose to allow the users to obfuscate the token embeddings before transmission and utilize local models for denoising. Nonetheless, transmitting the token embeddings and deploying local models may result in excessive communication and computation overhead, preventing practical implementation. In this work, we propose \textbf{DEL}, a framework for \textbf{D}ifferentially private and communication \textbf{E}fficient \textbf{L}LM split inference. More specifically, an embedding projection module and a differentially private stochastic quantization mechanism are proposed to reduce the communication overhead in a privacy-preserving manner. To eliminate the need for local models, we adapt soft prompt at the server side to compensate for the utility degradation caused by privacy. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work that utilizes soft prompt to improve the trade-off between privacy and utility in LLM inference, and extensive experiments on text generation and natural language understanding benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [25] [Stop Tracking Me! Proactive Defense Against Attribute Inference Attack in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11528)
*Dong Yan,Jian Liang,Ran He,Tieniu Tan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TRACE-RPS：结合细粒度匿名化与推理阻止优化的统一防御框架，将LLM属性推理准确率从约50%降至5%以下


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于匿名化的防御方法存在两个主要问题：1）粗粒度缺乏词级精度；2）仅修改用户文本仍无法阻止模型通过推理能力进行属性推断。需要一种能同时解决这两个限制的统一防御框架。

Method: 提出TRACE-RPS统一防御框架：1）TRACE利用注意力机制和推理链生成来识别和匿名化隐私泄露文本元素；2）RPS采用轻量级两阶段优化策略诱导模型拒绝行为，从而阻止属性推理。

Result: 在多种LLM上的评估显示，TRACE-RPS将属性推理准确率从约50%降至5%以下（开源模型）。此外，该方法展现出强大的跨模型泛化能力、提示变化鲁棒性以及效用-隐私权衡。

Conclusion: TRACE-RPS通过结合细粒度匿名化和推理阻止优化，有效防御LLM从用户生成文本中推断隐私属性，为大规模隐私泄露提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Recent studies have shown that large language models (LLMs) can infer private user attributes (e.g., age, location, gender) from user-generated text shared online, enabling rapid and large-scale privacy breaches. Existing anonymization-based defenses are coarse-grained, lacking word-level precision in anonymizing privacy-leaking elements. Moreover, they are inherently limited as altering user text to hide sensitive cues still allows attribute inference to occur through models' reasoning capabilities. To address these limitations, we propose a unified defense framework that combines fine-grained anonymization (TRACE) with inference-preventing optimization (RPS). TRACE leverages attention mechanisms and inference chain generation to identify and anonymize privacy-leaking textual elements, while RPS employs a lightweight two-stage optimization strategy to induce model rejection behaviors, thereby preventing attribute inference. Evaluations across diverse LLMs show that TRACE-RPS reduces attribute inference accuracy from around 50\% to below 5\% on open-source models. In addition, our approach offers strong cross-model generalization, prompt-variation robustness, and utility-privacy tradeoffs. Our code is available at https://github.com/Jasper-Yan/TRACE-RPS.

</details>


### [26] [LoRA-based Parameter-Efficient LLMs for Continuous Learning in Edge-based Malware Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11655)
*Christian Rondanini,Barbara Carminati,Elena Ferrari,Niccolò Lardo,Ashish Kundu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出基于LoRA适配器的边缘恶意软件检测持续学习架构，结合设备本地适应与全局知识共享，在资源受限的边缘设备上实现高效检测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备激增需要实时恶意软件检测方案，但现有方法面临挑战：静态或集中重训练模型难以应对演化威胁和异构流量；本地训练模型形成数据孤岛且跨域迁移能力差；大型语言模型资源需求过高不适合边缘部署

Method: 采用轻量级Transformer模型（DistilBERT、DistilGPT-2、TinyT5）在边缘节点运行，通过参数高效的LoRA适配器进行增量微调；仅聚合和重新分发LoRA模块，实现跨设备泛化而不交换原始数据；轻量级协调器管理知识共享

Result: 在Edge-IIoTset和TON-IoT数据集上评估，相比孤立微调，基于LoRA的交换在遇到来自其他域的未见攻击时获得20-25%的准确率提升；LoRA仅增加模型大小0.6-1.8MB（<1%）；在多轮学习中保持稳定的损失和F1分数

Conclusion: LoRA适配器为基础的持续学习架构有效解决了边缘恶意软件检测中的资源约束、数据隐私和跨域泛化问题，为边缘设备提供了实用且高效的实时安全解决方案

Abstract: The proliferation of edge devices has created an urgent need for security solutions capable of detecting malware in real time while operating under strict computational and memory constraints. Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in recognizing complex patterns, yet their deployment on edge devices remains impractical due to their resource demands. However, in edge malware detection, static or centrally retrained models degrade under evolving threats and heterogeneous traffic; locally trained models become siloed and fail to transfer across domains. To overcome these limitations, in this paper, we present a continuous learning architecture for edge-based malware detection that combines local adaptation on each device with global knowledge sharing through parameter-efficient LoRA adapters. Lightweight transformer models (DistilBERT, DistilGPT-2, TinyT5) run on edge nodes and are incrementally fine-tuned on device-specific traffic; only the resulting LoRA modules are aggregated by a lightweight coordinator and redistributed, enabling cross-device generalization without exchanging raw data. We evaluate on two public IoT security datasets, Edge-IIoTset and TON-IoT, under multi-round learning to simulate evolving threats. Compared to isolated fine-tuning, the LoRA-based exchange yields up to 20-25% accuracy gains when models encounter previously unseen attacks from another domain, while maintaining stable loss and F1 across rounds. LoRA adds less than 1% to model size (~0.6-1.8 MB), making updates practical for constrained edge hardware.

</details>


### [27] [More Haste, Less Speed: Weaker Single-Layer Watermark Improves Distortion-Free Watermark Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11793)
*Ruibo Chen,Yihan Wu,Xuehao Cui,Jingqi Zhang,Heng Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究发现传统"越强越好"的水印策略存在缺陷：强水印会降低token分布的熵，反而削弱多层水印集成的效果。提出使用较弱单层水印来保留熵，从而提高多层水印的检测性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统水印集成方法通常追求每层水印强度最大化，但作者发现这种"越强越好"的方法存在关键局限：强水印会显著降低token分布的熵，反而削弱后续层水印的有效性，导致信号衰减问题。

Method: 提出一个通用框架，使用较弱的单层水印来保留token分布所需的熵，从而支持有效的多层水印集成。该方法理论上和实证上都证明，通过控制水印强度来维持熵水平，可以避免多层集成中的信号衰减问题。

Result: 实证评估表明，这种反直觉的策略能够有效缓解信号衰减，在检测性和鲁棒性方面持续优于强基线方法。研究还从理论上证明了检测性受熵的限制，且水印集成会导致各层熵和绿表比率的单调下降。

Conclusion: 在多层水印集成中，并非水印强度越大越好。通过使用较弱的水印来保留token分布的熵，可以显著提高多层水印系统的整体性能，为解决水印集成中的信号衰减问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Watermarking has emerged as a crucial technique for detecting and attributing content generated by large language models. While recent advancements have utilized watermark ensembles to enhance robustness, prevailing methods typically prioritize maximizing the strength of the watermark at every individual layer. In this work, we identify a critical limitation in this "stronger-is-better" approach: strong watermarks significantly reduce the entropy of the token distribution, which paradoxically weakens the effectiveness of watermarking in subsequent layers. We theoretically and empirically show that detectability is bounded by entropy and that watermark ensembles induce a monotonic decrease in both entropy and the expected green-list ratio across layers. To address this inherent trade-off, we propose a general framework that utilizes weaker single-layer watermarks to preserve the entropy required for effective multi-layer ensembling. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that this counter-intuitive strategy mitigates signal decay and consistently outperforms strong baselines in both detectability and robustness.

</details>


### [28] [PAC to the Future: Zero-Knowledge Proofs of PAC Private Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11954)
*Guilhem Repetto,Nojan Sheybani,Gabrielle De Micheli,Farinaz Koushanfar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出结合PAC隐私与零知识证明的框架，在不可信计算环境中提供可验证的隐私保证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习系统对敏感用户数据的依赖增加引发隐私担忧，传统隐私保护技术在不可信计算环境中存在验证困难的问题

Method: 结合PAC隐私与零知识证明，使用非交互式ZKP方案生成证明，验证PAC隐私机制的正确实施

Result: 证明了在外部计算中实现可验证PAC隐私的可行性，为隐私保护机器学习和数据库系统提供实用解决方案

Conclusion: 该框架在保持计算完整性的同时，为不可信计算环境中的隐私保护提供了可验证的解决方案

Abstract: Privacy concerns in machine learning systems have grown significantly with the increasing reliance on sensitive user data for training large-scale models. This paper introduces a novel framework combining Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) Privacy with zero-knowledge proofs (ZKPs) to provide verifiable privacy guarantees in trustless computing environments. Our approach addresses the limitations of traditional privacy-preserving techniques by enabling users to verify both the correctness of computations and the proper application of privacy-preserving noise, particularly in cloud-based systems. We leverage non-interactive ZKP schemes to generate proofs that attest to the correct implementation of PAC privacy mechanisms while maintaining the confidentiality of proprietary systems. Our results demonstrate the feasibility of achieving verifiable PAC privacy in outsourced computation, offering a practical solution for maintaining trust in privacy-preserving machine learning and database systems while ensuring computational integrity.

</details>


### [29] [MedExChain: Enabling Secure and Efffcient PHR Sharing Across Heterogeneous Blockchains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12106)
*Yongyang Lv,Xiaohong Li,Kui Chen,Zhe Hou,Guangdong Bai,Ruitao Feng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MedExChain是一个跨链医疗数据共享方案，旨在安全地共享异构区块链系统中的个人健康记录，特别针对物联网医疗设备的性能限制进行了优化，并集成了密码学反向防火墙和区块链审计机制来防御内外安全威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能医疗系统的发展，物联网医疗设备实时生成的个人健康记录在疾病诊断中至关重要。区块链技术虽然增强了医疗系统的数据安全性，但不同区块链架构之间的数据共享仍面临性能和安全性挑战，限制了医疗数据的跨系统流通。

Method: 提出MedExChain跨链数据共享方案，该方案能够在物联网医疗设备性能限制下实现跨链PHR共享。方案集成了密码学反向防火墙（CRF）防御内部威胁，以及区块链审计机制防御外部威胁。通过BAN逻辑、Scyther工具、选择明文攻击（CPA）和算法替换攻击（ASA）安全分析验证方案鲁棒性。

Result: 广泛评估表明，MedExChain显著降低了计算和通信开销，使其适用于物联网医疗设备，并能促进个人健康记录在不同区块链系统中的高效流通。安全验证证明方案能够有效防御内外安全威胁。

Conclusion: MedExChain成功解决了异构区块链系统间医疗数据共享的性能和安全挑战，为智能医疗系统中的跨链数据流通提供了可行的解决方案，特别适合资源受限的物联网医疗设备环境。

Abstract: With the proliferation of intelligent healthcare systems, patients' Personal Health Records (PHR) generated by the Internet of Medical Things (IoMT) in real-time play a vital role in disease diagnosis. The integration of emerging blockchain technologies signiffcantly enhanced the data security inside intelligent medical systems. However, data sharing across different systems based on varied blockchain architectures is still constrained by the unsolved performance and security challenges. This paper constructs a cross-chain data sharing scheme, termed MedExChain, which aims to securely share PHR across heterogeneous blockchain systems. The MedExChain scheme ensures that PHR can be shared across chains even under the performance limitations of IoMT devices. Additionally, the scheme incorporates Cryptographic Reverse Firewall (CRF) and a blockchain audit mechanism to defend against both internal and external security threats. The robustness of our scheme is validated through BAN logic, Scyther tool, Chosen Plaintext Attack (CPA) and Algorithm Substitution Attack (ASA) security analysis veriffcation. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that MedExChain signiffcantly minimizes computation and communication overhead, making it suitable for IoMT devices and fostering the efffcient circulation of PHR across diverse blockchain systems.

</details>


### [30] [Keeping a Secret Requires a Good Memory: Space Lower-Bounds for Private Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12209)
*Alessandro Epasto,Xin Lyu,Pasin Manurangsi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 首次建立用户级差分隐私的无条件空间下界，通过多玩家通信博弈证明隐私算法需要Ω(T^{1/3})空间用于流中不同元素计数，相比非隐私算法的Õ(1)空间呈指数分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究差分隐私在内存效率方面的计算代价。虽然准确性与隐私之间的权衡已得到充分理解，但隐私在内存使用方面的固有代价仍未得到充分探索。

Method: 引入基于多玩家通信博弈的新证明技术，将低内存隐私算法的困难度与"贡献上限"（追踪和限制对数据集影响过大的用户）的必要性联系起来。将该框架应用于流中不同元素计数问题。

Result: 证明任何隐私算法需要几乎Õ(T^{1/3})空间才能达到特定错误率，解决了文献中的开放问题（Jain等，NeurIPS 2023和Cummings等，ICML 2025），建立了隐私算法与非隐私Õ(1)对应算法之间的首个指数空间复杂度分离。

Conclusion: 该通信理论技术可推广到广泛的问题类别，为隐私中位数、分位数和最大值选择等问题提供下界，首次建立了用户级差分隐私的无条件空间下界。

Abstract: We study the computational cost of differential privacy in terms of memory efficiency. While the trade-off between accuracy and differential privacy is well-understood, the inherent cost of privacy regarding memory use remains largely unexplored. This paper establishes for the first time an unconditional space lower bound for user-level differential privacy by introducing a novel proof technique based on a multi-player communication game.
  Central to our approach, this game formally links the hardness of low-memory private algorithms to the necessity of ``contribution capping'' -- tracking and limiting the users who disproportionately impact the dataset. We demonstrate that winning this communication game requires transmitting information proportional to the number of over-active users, which translates directly to memory lower bounds.
  We apply this framework, as an example, to the fundamental problem of estimating the number of distinct elements in a stream and we prove that any private algorithm requires almost $\widetildeΩ(T^{1/3})$ space to achieve certain error rates in a promise variant of the problem. This resolves an open problem in the literature (by Jain et al. NeurIPS 2023 and Cummings et al. ICML 2025) and establishes the first exponential separation between the space complexity of private algorithms and their non-private $\widetilde{O}(1)$ counterparts for a natural statistical estimation task. Furthermore, we show that this communication-theoretic technique generalizes to broad classes of problems, yielding lower bounds for private medians, quantiles, and max-select.

</details>


### [31] [Legitimate Overrides in Decentralized Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12260)
*Oghenekaro Elem,Nimrod Talmon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文开发了一个紧急干预机制的分类法，分析去中心化协议中应急架构的设计空间，通过实证数据验证了三种可测试的预测，并提出了从意识形态辩论转向定量工程的设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化协议声称具有不可变的、基于规则的执行，但许多协议嵌入了紧急机制（如链级冻结、协议暂停、账户隔离）。这些覆盖机制对于应对漏洞利用和系统性故障至关重要，但暴露了一个核心矛盾：干预何时能维护信任，何时被视为非法自由裁量？由于约100亿美元的技术漏洞损失可能通过链上干预解决（2016-2026），这些机制的设计具有很高的实际意义，但目前的方法仍然是临时性的且充满意识形态争议。

Method: 开发了一个Scope × Authority分类法，沿着两个维度映射紧急架构的设计空间：干预的精确性和触发权限的集中度。将由此产生的权衡（持续的中心化成本 vs. 遏制速度和附带破坏）形式化为随机成本最小化问题，并推导出三种可测试的预测。针对705个已记录的漏洞利用事件评估这些预测。

Result: 发现遏制时间因权限类型而系统性变化；损失遵循重尾分布（α≈1.33），风险集中在罕见的灾难性事件中；社区情绪可测量地调节维护干预能力的有效成本。分析得出了具体的设计原则。

Conclusion: 该分析产生了具体的设计原则，使紧急治理从意识形态辩论转向定量工程。通过Scope × Authority分类法和实证验证，为去中心化协议中的紧急干预机制提供了系统化的设计框架和量化评估方法。

Abstract: Decentralized protocols claim immutable, rule-based execution, yet many embed emergency mechanisms such as chain-level freezes, protocol pauses, and account quarantines. These overrides are crucial for responding to exploits and systemic failures, but they expose a core tension: when does intervention preserve trust and when is it perceived as illegitimate discretion? With approximately $10$ billion in technical exploit losses potentially addressable by onchain intervention (2016--2026), the design of these mechanisms has high practical stakes, but current approaches remain ad hoc and ideologically charged. We address this gap by developing a Scope $\times$ Authority taxonomy that maps the design space of emergency architectures along two dimensions: the precision of the intervention and the concentration of trigger authority. We formalize the resulting tradeoffs of a standing centralization cost versus containment speed and collateral disruption as a stochastic cost-minimization problem; and derive three testable predictions. Assessing these predictions against 705 documented exploit incidents, we find that containment time varies systematically by authority type; that losses follow a heavy-tailed distribution ($α\approx 1.33$) concentrating risk in rare catastrophic events; and that community sentiment measurably modulates the effective cost of maintaining intervention capability. The analysis yields concrete design principles that move emergency governance from ideological debate towards quantitative engineering.

</details>

### [32] [interwhen: A Generalizable Framework for Verifiable Reasoning with Test-time Monitors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11202)
*Vishak K Bhat,Prateek Chanda,Ashmit Khandelwal,Maitreyi Swaroop,Vineeth N. Balasubramanian,Subbarao Kambhampati,Nagarajan Natarajan,Amit Sharma*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: Interwhen是一个测试时验证框架，可在推理过程中实时验证模型输出的有效性，无需等待最终答案或强制结构化步骤分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在物理世界部署或法律金融等高风险场景中，确保推理模型输出的有效性至关重要。现有技术要么依赖生成后验证的低效方法，要么通过外部步骤分解限制模型的解题策略，需要一种更灵活高效的验证方法。

Method: 提出元提示方法：识别部分解应满足的可验证属性，然后提示模型遵循自定义格式输出推理轨迹，使部分输出易于解析和检查。支持自我验证和外部验证两种模式。

Result: 自我验证模式下，在早期停止推理模型中取得最先进结果，准确率无损失；外部验证模式下，相比测试时缩放方法准确率提升10个百分点，确保100%可靠性，效率提升4倍。

Conclusion: Interwhen提供了一种有效的抽象框架，可在不限制模型推理能力的前提下实时验证和引导模型输出，为高风险场景中的可靠推理提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: We present a test-time verification framework, interwhen, that ensures that the output of a reasoning model is valid wrt. a given set of verifiers. Verified reasoning is an important goal in high-stakes scenarios such as deploying agents in the physical world or in domains such as law and finance. However, current techniques either rely on the generate-test paradigm that verifies only after the final answer is produced, or verify partial output through a step-extraction paradigm where the task execution is externally broken down into structured steps. The former is inefficient while the latter artificially restricts a model's problem solving strategies. Instead, we propose to verify a model's reasoning trace as-is, taking full advantage of a model's reasoning capabilities while verifying and steering the model's output only when needed. The key idea is meta-prompting, identifying the verifiable properties that any partial solution should satisfy and then prompting the model to follow a custom format in its trace such that partial outputs can be easily parsed and checked. We consider both self-verification and external verification and find that interwhen provides a useful abstraction to provide feedback and steer reasoning models in each case. Using self-verification, interwhen obtains state-of-the-art results on early stopping reasoning models, without any loss in accuracy. Using external verifiers, interwhen obtains 10 p.p. improvement in accuracy over test-time scaling methods, while ensuring 100% soundness and being 4x more efficient. The code for interwhen is available at https://github.com/microsoft/interwhen

</details>


### [33] [Compositionality of Systems and Partially Ordered Runs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11203)
*Peter Fettke,Wolfgang Reisig*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 提出了处理Petri网及其运行的统一框架，用于组合和分解Petri网，证明了组合网的运行集等于各网运行组合的结果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Petri在1970年代后期引入部分有序事件发生（运行）作为描述分布式系统个体演化的适当模型，需要建立统一的框架来处理Petri网及其运行的组合与分解

Method: 提出了一个统一的框架来处理Petri网及其运行（部分有序事件发生），特别关注Petri网的组合和分解操作

Result: 证明了对于网M和N，组合网M·N的运行集等于M和N的运行组合

Conclusion: 该框架为Petri网及其运行的组合与分解提供了统一的理论基础，验证了组合操作的语义一致性

Abstract: In the late 1970s, C.A. Petri introduced partially ordered event occurrences (runs), then called \emph{processes}, as the appropriate model to describe the individual evolutions of distributed systems. Here, we present a unified framework for handling Petri nets and their runs, specifically to compose and decompose them. It is shown that, for nets $M$ and $N$, the set of runs of the composed net $M \bullet N$ equals the composition of the runs of $M$ and $N$.

</details>


### [34] [A Unified Treatment of Substitution for Presheaves, Nominal Sets, Renaming Sets, and so on](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11907)
*Fabian Lenke,Stefan Milius,Henning Urbat*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文在名义集合上引入闭幺半结构，统一建模替换操作，建立预层与名义集合之间的对应关系


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预层和名义集合都能为带自由变量和绑定变量的语法对象（如λ项）提供抽象模型。预层方法的一个优势是能通过闭幺半结构优雅地语法无关地刻画替换操作。本文旨在为名义集合引入类似的闭幺半结构，统一建模替换操作。

Method: 提出一种通用方法：从幺半范畴在某个范畴上的作用推导出该范畴的闭幺半结构。将此方法应用于名义集合及其相关范畴（如重命名集合），得到新的替换张量概念。该方法还能统一恢复预层范畴中已知的各种替换张量。

Result: 成功为名义集合及其相关范畴引入了闭幺半结构，建立了预层范畴与名义集合之间的新颖对应关系。该方法不仅统一恢复了预层范畴中已知的替换张量，还为名义集合和重命名集合等范畴产生了新的替换张量概念。

Conclusion: 本文为名义集合引入了闭幺半结构，统一了预层和名义集合对替换操作的建模。提出的通用方法建立了不同范畴间的对应关系，为语法对象的抽象模型提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Presheaves and nominal sets provide alternative abstract models of sets of syntactic objects with free and bound variables, such as lambda-terms. One distinguishing feature of the presheaf-based perspective is its elegant syntax-free characterization of substitution using a closed monoidal structure. In this paper, we introduce a corresponding closed monoidal structure on nominal sets, modeling substitution in the spirit of Fiore et al.'s substitution tensor for presheaves over finite sets. To this end, we present a general method to derive a closed monoidal structure on a category from a given action of a monoidal category on that category. We demonstrate that this method not only uniformly recovers known substitution tensors for various kinds of presheaf categories, but also yields novel notions of substitution tensor for nominal sets and their relatives, such as renaming sets. In doing so, we shed new light on different incarnations of nominal sets and (pre-)sheaf categories and establish a number of novel correspondences between them.

</details>


### [35] [Unravelling Abstract Cyclic Proofs into Proofs by Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12054)
*Lide Grotenhuis,Daniël Otten*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 将循环证明转化为带有良基归纳原理的有限证明，保持证明结构不变


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调和循环证明与传统有限证明之间的矛盾，为循环证明提供严格的数学基础

Method: 使用带标注的循环证明表示法，提取归纳假设并确定引入顺序；引入重置证明的改进版本，要求名称在重置前以统一方式覆盖

Result: 成功将任何循环证明转化为扩展系统中的有限证明，且保持证明结构；框架足够通用以处理满足大小变化终止原则的递归函数

Conclusion: 通过引入良基归纳原理，循环证明可以严格地转化为传统有限证明，为循环证明理论提供了坚实的理论基础

Abstract: Cyclic proof theory breaks tradition by allowing certain infinite proofs: those that can be represented by a finite graph, while satisfying a soundness condition. We reconcile cyclic proofs with traditional finite proofs: we extend abstract cyclic proof systems with a well-founded induction principle, and transform any cyclic proof into a finite proof in the extended system. Moreover, this transformation preserves the structure of the cyclic proof.
  Our results leverage an annotated representation of cyclic proofs, which allows us to extract induction hypotheses and to determine their introduction order. The representation is essentially a reset proof with one key modification: names must be covered in a uniform way before a reset. This innovation allows us to handle cyclic proofs where the underlying inductive sort is non-linear.
  Our framework is general enough to cover recursive functions satisfying the size-change termination principle, which are viewed as cyclic proofs under the Curry-Howard correspondence.

</details>


### [36] [Computing Distinguishing Formulae for Threshold-Based Behavioural Distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12084)
*Jonas Forster,Lutz Schröder,Paul Wild,Barbara König,Pedro Nora*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个统一框架，用于处理由将二值谓词提升为定量谓词的模态算子诱导的行为距离和逻辑，并以马尔可夫链的ε-双模拟距离为典型示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为距离通常比二值行为等价提供更细粒度的定量系统比较方法。它们常与定量模态相关，这些模态生成定量模态逻辑，通过诱导的逻辑距离来刻画给定的行为距离。需要开发一个统一框架来处理由特殊类型模态算子诱导的行为距离和逻辑。

Method: 开发了一个统一框架，处理由将二值谓词提升为定量谓词的模态算子诱导的行为距离和逻辑。该框架以马尔可夫链的ε-双模拟距离为典型示例，该距离最近被证明与由流行的Lévy-Prokhorov距离在分布上诱导的行为距离一致。其他示例包括度量转移系统上的行为距离和模糊转移系统上的Hausdorff行为距离。

Result: 主要通用结果涉及在两个特征模态逻辑中多项式时间提取区分公式：一个具有"满足度达ε"概念的二值逻辑，以及一个定量模态逻辑。这些结果在许多提到的示例中实例化为新结果。特别地，获得了马尔可夫链ε-双模拟距离在定量逻辑中的多项式时间区分公式提取，该逻辑包含用于概率知识表示的"一般"模态。

Conclusion: 该论文提出了一个统一框架，用于处理由提升二值谓词到定量谓词的模态算子诱导的行为距离和逻辑。主要贡献是在两个特征模态逻辑中多项式时间提取区分公式的通用结果，这些结果在许多具体系统中实例化为新的理论成果，特别是在马尔可夫链的ε-双模拟距离分析中。

Abstract: Behavioural distances generally offer more fine-grained means of comparing quantitative systems than two-valued behavioural equivalences. They often relate to quantitative modalities, which generate quantitative modal logics that characterize a given behavioural distance in terms of the induced logical distance. We develop a unified framework for behavioural distances and logics induced by a special type of modalities that lift two-valued predicates to quantitative predicates. A typical example is the probability operator, which maps a two-valued predicate $A$ to a quantitative predicate on probability distributions assigning to each distribution the respective probability of $A$. Correspondingly, the prototypical example of our framework is $ε$-bisimulation distance of Markov chains, which has recently been shown to coincide with the behavioural distance induced by the popular Lévy-Prokhorov distance on distributions. Other examples include behavioural distance on metric transition systems and Hausdorff behavioural distance on fuzzy transition systems. Our main generic results concern the polynomial-time extraction of distinguishing formulae in two characteristic modal logics: A two-valued logic with a notion of satisfaction up to $ε$, and a quantitative modal logic. These results instantiate to new results in many of the mentioned examples. Notably, we obtain polynomial-time extraction of distinguishing formulae for $ε$-bisimulation distance of Markov chains in a quantitative logic featuring a `generally' modality used in probabilistic knowledge representation.

</details>

### [37] [Calibration and Evaluation of Car-Following Models for Autonomous Shuttles Using a Novel Multi-Criteria Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11517)
*Renan Favero,Lily Elefteriadou*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 该研究通过校准多种跟驰模型（包括8种机器学习算法和2种物理模型）并使用真实自动驾驶班车轨迹数据，提出了一个多标准评估框架，发现XGBoost模型表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶班车（AS）具有与传统自动驾驶车辆不同的运行特性，但缺乏基于现场数据的专用跟驰模型校准研究。机器学习技术在AS轨迹分析中的应用尚未探索，且缺乏统一的跟驰模型评估框架。

Method: 使用真实世界AS轨迹数据校准了8种机器学习算法和2种物理模型，并提出了一个集成预测准确性、轨迹稳定性和统计相似性度量的多标准评估框架。

Result: 校准的XGBoost模型整体表现最佳；序列模型类型（如LSTM和CNN）能捕捉长期位置稳定性但对短期动态响应较差；传统模型（IDM、ACC）和核方法在准确性和稳定性方面低于大多数测试的ML模型。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了AS专用跟驰模型校准的空白，证明了机器学习在捕捉AS动态特性方面的潜力，并提供了一个可推广的跟驰模型系统评估方法学。

Abstract: Autonomous shuttles (AS) are fully autonomous transit vehicles with operating characteristics distinct from conventional autonomous vehicles (AV). Developing dedicated car-following models for AS is critical to understanding their traffic impacts; however, few studies have calibrated such models with field data. More advanced machine learning (ML) techniques have not yet been applied to AS trajectories, leaving the potential of ML for capturing AS dynamics unexplored and constraining the development of dedicated AS models. Furthermore, there is a lack of a unified framework for systematically evaluating and comparing the performance of car-following models to replicate real trajectories. Existing car-following studies often rely on disparate metrics, which limit reproducibility and performance comparability.
  This study addresses these gaps through two main contributions: (1) the calibration of a diverse set of car-following models using real-world AS trajectory data, including eight machine learning algorithms and two physics-based models; and (2) the introduction of a multi-criteria evaluation framework that integrates measures of prediction accuracy, trajectory stability, and statistical similarity, which provides a generalizable methodology for a systematic assessment of car-following models.
  Results indicated that the proposed calibrated XGBoost model achieved the best overall performance. Sequential model type, such as LSTM and CNN, captured long-term positional stability but were less responsive to short-term dynamics. LSTM and CNN captured long-term positional stability but were less responsive to short-term dynamics. Traditional models (IDM, ACC) and kernel methods showed lower accuracy and stability than most ML models tested.

</details>
